| **T1** The corpus this stage can be judged on | ✅ 2026-09-29 | **Two generators and a checker.** `corpus/make-text.py` writes ten fixtures into `ordinary/` — `columns-two`, `columns-three`, `table-ruled`, `rtl-text`, `cjk-vertical`, `hyphenated`, `text-tounicode`, `cjk-readable`, `search-classes`, `text-900` — and beside each its **ground truth** in `labels/`: every run of text in content order, blocks with roles in reading order, columns, table grids and the negatives a table detector is measured against, horizontal extents exact from the Helvetica widths (measured from Arial through Pillow once, written in as constants). `corpus/make-compare.py` writes **17 PDFs in 14 pairs** into `compare/` with `<name>.expected.json` change records, and a self-test that re-derives every revised model by applying the records with independent code. `corpus/fixturecheck.py` is A-14's byte check. `fontenc.py` gains thirteen **approximate** Hebrew letters. `--verify` now covers `labels/*.json` and `compare/*.json`, orphans included. **Measured:** `--verify` **121 files** (97 PDFs, 24 JSON) byte-identical; `--check-provenance` 97 rows and every sidecar resolving; `--self-test` **10 of 10** controls; `--check-faults` clean; `make-compare.py --self-test` **14 pairs, 4 of 4** controls; `--check-manifest` *"97 corpus files, 92 with baselines and 5 excluded"*; **84 baselines** captured in one filtered run, no tracked PNG changed and all 231 earlier capture entries identical; `ctest` **1149/1149 on `x64-release` and on `x64-debug`**, the same three named skips, the +2 being `gates.corpus.faults` and `gates.corpus.compare`; `gates` **129/129**; `checks` clean and **`mutation-lint` one command** — D5-1's invariant on the stage's first day. `tests/baselines/stage4-signoff-test-names.txt` holds the 1,147 names A-109 checks at T14. Guide A-12 … A-16 ✅. **Deviations:** **(a) `cjk-readable.pdf` is an eleventh fixture**, split out of `text-tounicode.pdf`: D5-23's readable CJK was **measured to render** with the machine's face (`STSong -> SimSun`), and the capture records a substitution per DOCUMENT, so as a third page it made `text-tounicode.pdf`'s Latin baseline machine-dependent — per-page resources did not change that (measured). It is the third `machineDependent` golden case. **(b) Two fixtures were corrected after they were LOOKED AT**, every new page having been rendered into a scratch directory before capture: the report pair's text fitted in one column of what is meant to be a two-column report, and its table's first column was too narrow. The Hebrew was checked by enlarging a line, since its ORDER is the fixture. **(c) `.gitattributes`** gains `corpus/labels/*.json` and `corpus/compare/*.json` as text: the existing `corpus/*.json` rule is anchored and had left them `binary`. **(d)** `golden/README.md`'s census re-derived (315 PNGs, 92 files, 105 pages, 279 grey), and it now names three machine-dependent cases |
| **T2** `PageTextModel`, `PdfiumTextExtractor` and the engine thread's text job | ✅ 2026-09-30 | **The model, the extractor, the job.** `core/text/PageTextModel` holds one entry per Unicode scalar — loose box and origin in unrotated page space, angle, font (a deduplicated table of name, flags, weight) and size, and the **generated**, **hyphen** and **unmapped** flags — and **enforces that an unmapped glyph is stored as U+FFFD**, whatever the engine guessed; `hasExtractableText()` is the question every consumer asks first. `pdf/render/PdfiumTextExtractor` fills it inside the engine lock, one font lookup per text object, joining surrogate pairs; it refuses a foreign handle and leaves the model untouched on any failure. `DocumentBackendImpl` hands one out per document — **the census is 4 of 7**. `core/render/RenderScheduler` gains **a second kind of job on the same thread** (D5-3): `enqueueText(TextRequest, lane)`, a fourth lane **`Background`** below `Speculative`, the same tickets, coalescing, raising and cancellation, **no revision and no supersession** (the engine never reloads, and the text is in page space), never the tile cache, never the latency probe, and **a failure delivered** rather than dropped. `app/BackendTextExtractor.h` is the per-worker adapter, and `CompositionRoot` passes it as the scheduler's second factory. One catalog key, `error.text.extractFailed`, in both locales. **Measured (guide A-17 … A-26):** A-17 — 620 generated lines and 40,718 characters over 17 labelled pages, 335 content runs over 6 more, nothing invented or missing; A-18 — 65,939 glyphs and lines inside their boxes at 144 dpi, 0 outside, the 2 pt control 65,934 outside; A-22 — 3,064 glyphs over 7 pages × 4 view rotations through `PagePresentation`'s mapping, 0 outside, the quarter-turn control 3,064; A-26 — 97 files, 2,103 pages, 11,957,020 characters, 90 unmapped, 0 control characters. **What the engine does, recorded rather than assumed:** it decomposes U+FB00…U+FB06; it replaces a line-end hyphen's codepoint with **U+0002** (stored as U+002D); it reports a base-14 font's weight as **0** (stored as −1, bold read from the flags or the standard name); it returns a `/Rotate 90` page's characters in a different order; and **its right-to-left order is neither logical nor visual** — each word's letters logical, the words in left-to-right page order, identically for both emissions — so **D5-6's second branch applies and T4's analyzer reorders**. `ctest` **1172/1172 on `x64-release` and on `x64-debug`**, the same three named skips (+23: `tst_textmodel` 5, the scheduler's text cases 7, `tst_extract` 11), 0 `warning:` in the debug build of the new files; `gates` **129/129**; `checks` clean, `mutation-lint` **one command** (D5-1), `layer-lint` 432 files and the same 14 escapes. **Planted controls, each fired and restored:** the hyphen replacement removed (A-20 ×3, A-26), the U+FFFD rule removed (the model case, A-21 ×90), `cancelDocument()` blind to text (the scheduler case), the handle check disabled (A-25). Guide A-17, A-18, A-19, A-20, A-22, A-25, A-26 ✅; **A-21, A-23, A-24 🟡**, their T2 halves recorded and the rest owed to T5/T6 (consumers, callers' lanes) and T4 (the reorder). **Deviations:** **(a)** A-17 grades `text-a4`, `a2-text-page` and `pages-900` — which have no label — against the runs in their content streams, read first-party through `pdf/cos/`, never PDFium. **(b)** A-21's wording is corrected: `cjk-embedded.pdf`'s captions are text, so the consumers' claim is about its 90 glyphs, not the page. **(c)** Two existing cases asserting a null extractor were corrected with their **names kept** for A-109. **(d)** A-22's oracle leaves out the 16 glyphs the generator's arrow stroke crosses, found as 16 misses on its first run. **CI:** run **36706046676** on `a3a1e32`, green (reported by the user 2026-09-30). |
| **T3** A2 at Stage 5, and O7's revisit | ✅ 2026-09-30 — **A2 AT STAGE 5: `go, with named custom work`** (the user's choice; **re-graded `go` by the user at T14, 2026-10-02**); **O7 keeps its resolution** | **The measurement.** `bench_pipeline --stage5` (phase 6, D5-22): after phase 0's latency control, a 1920×1080 viewport at 288 dpi on a fresh page of `text-900.pdf` each round while the background works — the whole 900-page index, a folder search over a scratch copy of the corpus (88 files opened off the pump), and a 900-page comparison's text plus 72-dpi rasters — at the shipped 256 MiB cap and at 24 MiB, background fed 32 jobs at a time. Timing wrappers stamp every job on the worker, which is what the per-page extraction figures and the inversion count are read from; `bench.render.a2_stage5` registers it. **Measured (two runs, `Tech-Stack-Plan.md` §3.3):** viewport p95 — viewer 13.02–13.07, index build 14.05–14.26 (six figures), comparison 13.67–13.76, **folder search 17.68–17.93 ms**; one page's extraction max **2.50–2.65 ms**; **0 inversions**, 0 overflow, **text inserted nothing into the tile cache** and 0 cross-document evictions — O7's revisit, recorded in its §8 row. **The folder search is the named condition, and its cause is OPENING, found by measuring rather than guessed:** `PdfiumDocument::open` loads every page — ~145 ms of engine for 900 pages — and the unfair `std::mutex` let the open take the lock back ahead of the waiting worker, so the first runs had a worst tile of **131–260 ms**; per-page release and `yield()` changed nothing. **Built in T3:** the engine lock is a **first-come, first-served ticket lock** that records its owner, and the page walk takes it per page (worst tile **23.5–24.8 ms**, guide I-02); **named for T9:** a folder search opens files on the engine thread at `Background` in pieces no longer than one extraction (**D5-13 amended**). **Also built:** `RenderScheduler::enqueue(…, CachePolicy::Bypass)` — D5-4's *"the comparison's rasters are never inserted into the tile cache"* had no mechanism; a bypassing tile is neither looked up nor kept. **Found and fixed: guide I-01 (S1, latent)** — the outline, destination and Info readers entered PDFium **without the engine lock** from Stage 4 T4, on the UI thread beside the render worker; `FPDF_GetLastError` likewise. Now under the lock, and `requireEngineLock()` counts any entry without it. `ctest` **1176/1176 on `x64-release` and `x64-debug`** (+4: the bypass case, I-01's case, the fairness case, `bench.render.a2_stage5`), the same three skips, 0 `warning:` in the debug build; `gates` 129/129; `checks` clean, `mutation-lint` one command. **Planted controls, each fired and restored:** the readers' lock removed (I-01's case: 12 unlocked entries), an unfair engine lock (the fairness case failed 3 of 3). Guide **A-27 … A-31 ✅**. **Deviations:** **(a)** `CachePolicy::Bypass` built here rather than with the comparison (T12), because A-30 cannot be measured without it. **(b)** The engine lock's type changed to `EngineMutex` — every `lock_guard` over it names the new type. **(c)** The first run counted **1** inversion that was the instrument's: the reference was stamped before `enqueue()` returned; it is now stamped after. **(d)** Two of my bench runs were left running concurrently by a truncated pipeline and were stopped; every figure recorded here comes from runs made alone. |
| **T4** `TextNormalizer` and `LayoutAnalyzer`, measured | ✅ 2026-09-30 — **the layout baseline meets every §3.2 target** | **`core/text/TextNormalizer`** (D5-5): one pipeline per cluster — NFKC, simple case folding, diacritics stripped on request — with an offset map from every output unit to the first and last model character it came from, so a hit maps to whole glyphs; line-final hyphens joined before a lowercase letter (PDFium's flag), kept with no space otherwise; unmapped glyphs produce nothing a query can match. **`core/text/LayoutAnalyzer`** (D5-6), geometry only and never the model's order: vertical CJK lines, baseline bands, lines split at 0.8 em with word gaps below, right-to-left lines reordered by UAX #9 L1–L2 over resolved levels, ruled and unruled tables, blocks by leading, list markers, XY-cut order, columns; non-finite geometry reported unplaced; bounded throughout (`kMaxCharsLaidOut`, recent-block window, cut depth). `PageTextModel` gained **`rules()`** — the page's thin paths, which the extractor now records — because D5-6's ruled tables need ruling lines and a model of characters has none. **`fuzz_text_pipeline`** (D5-21) with its body in `tests/fuzz/TextPipelineFuzzBody.h`, replayed on Windows over 3,000 adversarial inputs; the fuzz count **3 → 4**, `gates.fuzz_count.four_of_four_with_a_helper` and `…three_of_four_fails` added, the three-target cases kept; `ci.yml` and `fuzz-soak.yml` run it seeded from `corpus/labels/`. `tests/render/TextOracle.h` now holds T2's label oracle so the layout grading uses the same claim. **Measured (A-38, 17 labelled pages):** line F1 **1.000** (620 of 620), block F1 **1.000** (104 of 104), columns **17 of 17 pages**, reading order **1.000** (87 of 87 pairs), tables precision and recall **1.00** (2 of 2, none invented from the list that looks like one), grids **2 of 2** — recorded in R1's row (`Tech-Stack-Plan.md` §10) with its bounds: our own generated corpus, and **not out of sample**, the thresholds having been chosen beside the generator. **Found by measuring, and fixed:** (a) PDFium gives a space drawn on its own a zero-size box on the baseline, which made each such space a line of its own on `rtl-text.pdf`'s logical page — a degenerate box now takes its height from origin and size; (b) **guide I-03 (S3)**: `corpus/make-text.py` labelled `columns-two.pdf` page 2 as two columns when its body text is in one — corrected on the user's decision to count columns that hold text, one label line changed and no PDF (`--verify` 121 byte-identical). `ctest` **1198/1198 on `x64-release` and `x64-debug`** (+22: `tst_normalize` 6, `tst_layout` 8, `tst_layoutcorpus` 6, two fuzz-count gates), the same three skips, 0 `warning:` in the debug build; `gates` **131/131**; `checks` clean, `mutation-lint` one command. **Planted controls, each fired and restored:** the right-to-left reorder removed (A-23 and the unit case), the hyphen join removed (A-36, both levels), the short-cell table rule removed (prose columns became tables: precision 0.20, block F1 0.815). Guide A-23 now ✅, A-32 … A-39 and A-41 ✅; **A-40 🟡** until the Linux job's log is read. **Deviations:** **(a)** D5-5 clarified (a hard hyphen before a capital stays, no space). **(b)** `PageTextModel::rules()` and the extractor's path pass are new. **(c)** A-33's recorded behaviour holds: Qt's `toCaseFolded` is simple folding, `ß` ≠ `SS`. **(d)** A-39's shuffle is asserted on synthetic models (`tst_layout`) and `tagged.pdf` for stability; a tree's benefit is Stage 7's. |
| **T5** Selection and copy | ✅ 2026-10-01 — **I-04 raised and closed (S2)** | **`core/text/TextSelection`** (D5-8, D5-9), pure and headless: `PageReading` is one page in `LayoutAnalyzer`'s reading order with every position's line and block beside it; `DocumentText` the pages a selection has read; a FLOW selection is two carets over the DOCUMENT's reading order, across pages, and a COLUMN a rectangle on one page taking the spans of every line it crosses. Hit-testing finds the nearest line and then the character, the caret on the side of its centre read in the character's OWN direction (a right-to-left letter reads from its right half); two, three and four clicks select the word, the line and the block. `copy()` writes plain text (lines as seen, a blank line between blocks and pages) and HTML (paragraphs, D5-5's hyphen rule, each run's family, size, bold and italic, the fragment between the standard markers) at once, and a column -- or a table block a flow meets -- as tab-separated text and a `<table>`; **an unmapped glyph is never copied** (D5-2): a selection of them alone copies nothing and is refused as `NotExtractable`. **`ui/viewer/SelectionOverlay`**, ONE per document window and handed to every pane of a split: it asks the engine thread for a page's text at `Visible` when a person selects on it and at `Background` for a copy's missing pages (D5-3), keeps a gesture as the POINTS it was made at until the text arrives, paints the highlight over the tiles (D4-11, never rasterized), and puts one `QMimeData` on Qt's clipboard; a pending copy shows progress in the status bar and `Esc` cancels it with the clipboard unchanged. **`ui/viewer/tools/SelectTool`** given its subject: a drag, a multi-click, `Alt`+drag (`Features.md` §8, Q2), `Shift`+click. `Tool.h` gains `PressContext` (modifiers, clicks) and `ToolHost::selection()`, `pagePointAt()` and `pagePointOn()`; `core/render/PageLayout::toPage` maps a content point into a NAMED page's space, outside it too. `edit.copy` and `edit.selectAll` registered at §8's Common layer -- **96 registered, 277 unregistered of 373** (`--list-actions`), **no id minted** (D5-9). Six catalog keys, `en` and `zh-TW`: five `shell.status.selection*` and `shell.tooltip.needsSelection`. **Measured:** A-42's highlight over `columns-two.pdf` against the rendered ink, *2136 glyphs judged, 113 selected, 0 outside, 0 intruding* (the 2 pt control 3 outside); A-44 across a page break 0 and 0 over 2746 glyphs; A-47 and A-48 per line; `Ctrl+A`, `Ctrl+C` over `text-900.pdf` -- *899 pages read at Background and 7,190,818 characters on the clipboard in 2363 ms*; **A-50 by hand on the `windows` platform: CF_HTML read back through Win32, 17 of 17** (D5-10 measured; `CF_UNICODETEXT` carries CR LF). **Found and fixed, guide I-04 (S2):** a READ-ONLY text view accepts Qt's `ShortcutOverride` for nothing, so once `edit.copy`/`edit.selectAll` were registered, `Ctrl+A` in the log pane selected the document, and Document Properties' values lost both keys; `ShortcutBinder::selectableTextOwnsKey` leaves Copy and Select All to any focused widget whose `textInteractionFlags` say its text can be selected -- one rule, by property and not by type -- and `LogPane.h`'s Stage 1 claim that it had them is corrected in place. **Tests:** `ctest` **1228/1228 on `x64-release` and on `x64-debug`** (+30: `tst_selection` 11, `tst_selecttool` 10, `tst_clipboard` 1, `tst_selectionink` 8), **four named skips** -- the three before and A-50's, which skips offscreen; `gates` **131/131**; `checks` clean, `mutation-lint` one command, `layer-lint` 14 escapes; `rawpage_ui_tests.exe` whole on both presets, 379 cases, 3 skipped (seeds 20261001 and 5150). **Six planted controls, each fired and restored:** an unmapped glyph copied; every caret read left to right; the column-crossing rule removed -- **green on its first run**, because every line of that case's page was crossed, so a caption outside the rectangle was added and it then failed (convention 22); I-04's rule removed; a selection change laying the view out and asking for tiles; `Esc` not clearing. Guide A-42 ... A-51, A-53 ... A-55 ticked, A-52 🟡 until T7's find bar joins it, A-21's copy half, A-24's selection lane and A-41's selection clause recorded. **Deviations:** (a) the ink cases are `tests/composition/tst_selectionink.cpp`, not `tst_selecttool` -- ink needs the engine and `rawpage_ui_tests` links none; `tests/ui/tst_selecttool.cpp` holds the widget cases over a fake text extractor the UI fixture gained; (b) A-42 over `columns-two.pdf`, not `text-a4.pdf`, which has no label to grade the text against; (c) **a selection's pages are held per DOCUMENT WINDOW, in `SelectionOverlay`'s own `DocumentText`** -- D5-4's shared, budgeted, `DocumentId`-keyed `core/text/DocumentIndex` is T6's and does not exist, so a `window.new` companion extracts its own; (d) D5-8 clarified the day it met Stage 4's temporary hand: a `Shift`+CLICK extends, a `Shift`+DRAG pans, `Alt` with a multi-click is the unit, a column stays on its page; (e) `SelectionOverlay`'s FileMap row listed drag handles and a selection context menu -- neither is built, and the row says so; (f) Stage 4's `the selection tool selects nothing and says so` keeps its name for A-109 and asserts what is still true plus that the drag now selects; Stage 1's `a text field keeps the keys it edits with` asserts the real registration with a control; two registration counts 94 -> 96; (g) each delivered page is laid out on the UI thread as it arrives -- not measured per page; M-02 step 3 judges a 900-page copy's responsiveness. **Lockstep paid here:** Stage 5's T2, T3 and T4 rows had been written into Stage 4's task table and are moved under T1. Manual M-01, M-02, M-04 and M-05 are walkable and not walked *(as of that task; every Stage 5 manual case passed by 2026-10-04, `Stage5-TextSearch-Testing-Guide.md` §6)*. |
| **T6** `SearchQuery`, `SearchEngine`, `DocumentIndex`; the Search keys and page | ✅ 2026-10-01 — **the second search of `text-900.pdf` is INSTANT by the guide's §3.3: p95 13.2 ms against 100** | **`core/text/DocumentIndex`** (D5-4) is **where extracted text lives**: keyed by `DocumentId` -- a window ATTACHES its handle under the backend's identity for it, and two handles over one identity are ONE entry, so `window.new`'s companion extracts nothing its leader has; a handle with no stated identity is an entry of its own. Each `IndexedPage` holds the engine's model and builds on first use, on whichever thread asks, the `PageReading` and one `SearchablePage` per normalization -- D5-5's pipeline cut at every BLOCK, its offsets turned into reading positions. Every text request goes through `request(owner, entry, page, lane)`: one ticket per page, RAISED for a more urgent owner, cancelled only when the LAST owner lets go, and **re-sent through the next handle when the one it went through detaches** (the leader closing under a companion). A mutation's `Scope` drops the pages it names. Its own budget, `search/indexCacheMB`: whole documents evicted least recently SEARCHED first, a never-searched one first of all, **the document being searched never evicted for itself** (D5-4 clarified), memory only, the entry dropped with its last handle. **`core/text/SearchEngine`**: a pure MATCH -- `compile()` (a literal through `TextNormalizer`'s query path; PCRE2 over a pattern NFKC-normalized and mark-stripped like the page and never case-folded, because folding `\S` makes `\s`; an empty pattern and an invalid regex REFUSED, `error.search.emptyPattern` / `error.search.invalidPattern` naming it) and `findOnPage()` block by block, whole words as UAX #29 without a dictionary (each ideograph a word, D5-5's caveat), a selection scope per page -- and an asynchronous RUN: the pages in scope asked of the index at `Background`, at most 32 at a time, matched on a pool of its own (1–4 threads, never the UI or the engine thread), delivered from `deliverReady()` on the pumping thread; `maxResults` keeps the first N in document order, says so, and stops asking once settled; unmapped glyphs match nothing and every page holding them is REPORTED; `SearchSession` cancels its previous run AFTER the new one has asked. **`core/text/SearchQuery.h`** with `fromSettings()`, the one reader of the flags. **`ui/viewer/SelectionOverlay` now reads its pages from the index** -- the relation T5 left open: its `DocumentText` is a view onto the index of the readings it USED, so a page another window or a search extracted costs it nothing, it never takes the pages a search indexed, it keeps its pages when the budget evicts them and drops them when a `Scope` invalidates them; `DocumentText::erase` added. `DocumentWindow` attaches, invalidates and detaches; `app/Services.h` gains `DocumentIndex&` with its consumers; `CompositionRoot` builds the index after the scheduler, destroys it before it and applies `search/indexCacheMB` live. **The ten `search/*` rows** (D5-20) and **the Search page -- nine of 26** (`shippedPages()`); eleven `prefs.*` keys and three `error.search.*` keys in `en` and `zh-TW`. `TextNormalizer` gained an ASCII fast path (the same output; three strings a character fewer). **Measured (A-63, `text-900.pdf`, 900 pages, 7,247,880 characters):** first search, the index build, 1,708 ms (not graded); twenty literal queries after it, 142,840 hits, **p95 13.23 ms** (12.48 on a second run), max 16.47; **the complete index 835 MB**, which the 1024 MB default holds (asserted). **Tests:** `ctest` **1276/1276 on `x64-release` and on `x64-debug`** (+48: `tst_search` 15, `tst_index` 19, `tst_stage5settings` 4, `unit.search_settings.restart` -- the ten values written by one process and read by another, an empty file its control -- `tst_searchcorpus` 5, `tst_documentindex` 4), the same **four named skips**; `gates` **131/131**; `checks` clean, **`mutation-lint` one command** (D5-1), `layer-lint` 14 escapes; `rawpage_ui_tests.exe` whole on both presets, 383 cases, 3 skipped (seeds 20261001 and 5150); a `--clean-first` `x64-debug` build, **0 `warning:` and 0 `error:` lines over 301 objects**. **Fifteen planted controls, each fired and restored:** the word boundary always true; the block cut removed; identity ignored; `invalidate` dropping nothing; the eviction order reversed; the session letting go before asking (72 asks for 40); the bound's early settling removed; the re-send removed; `release` ignoring other owners; the selection taking every arriving page; the unmapped count zeroed; the regex's case option removed; the index forgetting after each search; every delivery 150 ms late (**p95 172.8 ms**, the §3.3 grade failed); and matching on the pumping thread -- **green on its first plant**, which reached only pages already indexed while the case's search found none, so the case gained a second search and the plant moved to where every page passes, and it failed (convention 7). Guide A-56 … A-68 ticked, and A-21 and A-24 ticked whole with their T6 halves; A-41's search clause recorded. **Deviations:** (a) **`SearchEngine` is not composed into the product at T6**: its first widget is T7's find bar, and `app/Services.h` says a service arrives with its consumer -- so T6's search is reachable from tests and from the Preferences page's keys, and `tests/ui/tst_documentindex.cpp` drives one it owns over the fixture's index; (b) **D5-4 clarified**: the document being searched is kept when alone over the budget, said once per episode -- *"never exceeded after a search completes"* holds whenever it fits; (c) the four `search/include*` keys are declared and read by nothing until T8 (D5-12), as planned; (d) **found while moving the selection's store, before any run**: a copy whose every page the index already held would have waited for pages that never arrive -- `SelectionOverlay::copy` takes held pages first and completes at once; (e) `search/maxResults`' floor is 1 and its default 10,000, `search/indexCacheMB` 16 to 16,384 MB, default 1024 -- chosen at T6 and argued in `SettingsKeys.def`. Manual M-01, M-02, M-04 and M-05 are walkable and not walked *(as of that task; every Stage 5 manual case passed by 2026-10-04, `Stage5-TextSearch-Testing-Guide.md` §6)*; T6 adds no manual case. |
| **T7** The find bar | ✅ 2026-10-01 — **I-05 raised and closed (S3)** | **`ui/write/FindReplaceBar`, the find half** (D5-14), at the path the FileMap gives it although the rest of `ui/write/` is Stage 13's. **One per document window**, above the panes and hidden until `Ctrl+F`: it searches that tab's `DocumentIndex` entry and paints through that tab's `SelectionOverlay`, which every pane of a split shares. It declares `KeyContext::FindSearch`. **Incremental:** each edit restarts a 200 ms debounce, the search starts at the pause, and a newer one cancels the older through `SearchSession`; `Enter` starts a pending search at once. The current hit is the first at or after the page the reader is on, and the view goes to it. **Its toggles ARE the settings:** the three buttons dispatch `find.toggleRegex`, `find.toggleWholeWords` and `find.toggleCaseSensitive`, which write `search/*`, and the bar subscribes to the Search group and re-runs -- it keeps no copy. Every button dispatches its command through `ActionRegistry`, and none carries a `QAction` (convention 23). Scope: the whole document, the selection, or a page range (`3-5`). **It says** the count, the progress of a first search, *no matches*, a wrap past either end, the bound, and always the glyphs in scope with no Unicode; **an invalid regular expression is the error itself, and nothing is searched.** `SelectionOverlay` gains **search hits**, painted under the selection in two colours -- every hit, and the current one deeper -- and never rasterized (D4-11); `searchHitRect()` is a hit's page-space rectangle. `PageView::revealPageRect()` scrolls the least it can to show a rectangle, a third of the way down, and not at all when it is on screen. **The eight Find / Search commands registered** -- `find.find` (§8's Common and FindSearch `Ctrl+F`), `find.next`, `find.previous`, `find.jumpToResult`, `find.close` and the three toggles -- **104 registered, 269 unregistered of 373** (`--list-actions`), **no id minted**; `find.advancedSearch` is T9's. **`SearchEngine` composed into the product**: `CompositionRoot` builds it after the index, pumps it with its own wake handler as it pumps the scheduler, and destroys it before the index; `app/Services.h` gains `SearchEngine&`, and `ShellFixture` pumps it. Sixteen `search.bar.*` keys in `en` and `zh-TW`, and `search` joins `i18n-lint`'s `keyFamilies`. **Found and fixed, guide I-05 (S3):** the sixteen entries were written inside the `prefs` `<context>`, where a lookup by family finds nothing, and the bar showed `search.bar.count` -- every check passed it; **`i18n-lint` gains the blocking rule `wrong-context`**, whose first run found **`shell.tooltip.needsPresentation` in the `action` context since Stage 4 T10**, moved to `shell`; `gates.i18n_lint.wrong_context` and `ui.every key the shell can put on screen resolves to text and not to itself` are the regressions. **Measured, by a person's path:** over `columns-two.pdf`, `viewport` typed into the bar -- *1290 glyphs judged against their ink, 32 selected, 0 outside the highlight, 0 inside it that should not be*, the 2 pt control 4 outside, and no tile rasterized for the search (A-74). **Tests:** `ctest` **1292/1292 on `x64-release` and on `x64-debug`** (+16: `tst_findbar` 12, two `ui.composition.` cases, the resolve case, `gates.i18n_lint.wrong_context`), four named skips; `gates` **132/132**; `checks` clean, `mutation-lint` one command, `layer-lint` 14 escapes; `rawpage_ui_tests.exe` whole on both presets, 396 cases, 3 skipped (seeds 20261001 and 5150); a `--clean-first` `x64-debug` build with its log KEPT, **0 `warning:` and 0 `error:` lines over 303 objects** -- the first clean build of the run counted 13 `warning:` lines in a log that was not kept, the touched files recompiled warn-free, and the kept second build had none, so their source is not established (that first build also re-ran the configure this task's CMake changes forced, and PowerShell's `Select-String` matches case-insensitively). **Eleven planted controls, each fired and restored:** no debounce; the wrap message removed; `Esc` leaving the highlights; the toggles not following the settings; an invalid pattern read as *no matches*; the bar declaring no context; stepping without revealing; *highlight all* ignored; a hit highlighted past its end -- **green on its first plant**, one character past `viewport` being a space with no ink, so it moved two and failed (convention 7); the `wrong-context` rule off; one `search` entry back in `prefs`. Guide A-69 … A-75 ticked, A-52 ticked whole, A-57's and A-21's bar halves recorded. **Deviations:** (a) **hits do not stream** while a first search builds the index: its progress is shown and its hits arrive when it finishes (1.7 s for `text-900.pdf`, A-63) -- §5.4's *"hits stream in as pages complete"* is not built; (b) **`Enter` is §8's literal "jump to the highlighted result"** -- it reveals the current hit and does not step, `F3` does; (c) `F3` and its aliases are FindSearch rows, so with the focus back on the page they do nothing -- as §8 binds them; (d) opening the bar shortens the page view by its height, which re-tiles it -- recorded at A-74; (e) A-89, `Ctrl+Shift+F` with the focus in the bar, waits for T9's pane. Manual **M-06 is walkable** and not walked, beside M-01, M-02, M-04 and M-05. |
| **T8** The other four content classes | ✅ 2026-10-01 — **each class searched only with its switch on, over `search-classes.pdf` through the real engine; D5-12 clarified** | **One read-only facade reader** (D5-12): `IDocumentBackend::searchUnits(handle, part, into)` hands back comments, form fields, bookmarks and attachments as plain **search units** (`core/text/SearchUnit.h`: a class, which string of its object, the text, its page or none, a page-space rectangle, a locator, a bookmark's destination) -- no Stage 6 or Stage 9 model built. **Read in PARTS, each no longer than one page's work** (D5-12 clarified): a page's annotations, the outline's titles, the attachments -- because one job walking every page would hold a visible tile for ~145 ms on `text-900.pdf`, which D5-13's amendment forbids for an open. Each part runs on the engine thread as an **engine task**, a third kind of job on `RenderScheduler` (`enqueueTask`, `subscribeTasks`: the text job's queue, priority, tickets and cancellation, `cancelDocument()` in all three states, never coalesced, cached or probed). `pdf/render/PdfiumDocument::searchUnits` reads a page's MARKUP annotations' `/Contents` and `/Subj` (ISO 32000-1 12.5.6.2 -- not a link, a popup or a widget) and its widgets' qualified field names and values, each with its `/Rect`, under one lock; the outline from what open read; and each attachment's name, its description and -- for a PDF -- each page's text, opened from bytes by `openBytes` with no password callback, **one level deep**, the one long part (a bound recorded at the reader). **`core/text/DocumentIndex` keeps the parts** beside the pages: `requestUnits()` mirrors `request()`, each part read once, stored failed rather than retried, dropped with its page by a `Scope`, counted in the budget and evicted with the document; `app/CompositionRoot` wires `setUnitReader()` to the backend. **`SearchQuery` gains `SearchClasses`**, read by `fromSettings()` -- the four `search/include*` keys, read by nothing since T6 -- and **`SearchEngine`** matches a run's SLOTS: each page once its text and (comments or fields on) its unit part are held, and for a whole-document search including bookmarks or attachments one document slot ahead of the pages; `findInUnit()` matches a unit's string through `TextNormalizer::normalizeString` -- the page's own pipeline over a model of the string -- with the page's match loop; hits in document order, the document's units first, a field shown by several widgets ONE hit; a page range searches the comments and fields on its pages only, a selection page text only; a slot waiting for one piece re-asks for one the index dropped meanwhile. **The find bar** navigates as D5-12 says: a comment or a field to its page and rectangle (`SelectionOverlay` paints and reveals a unit hit by its `/Rect`), a bookmark through `destinationActivated` to `PageView::goToDestination`, and **an attachment hit is selected and opens nothing**; its status line names the class -- four `search.bar.in*` keys in `en` and `zh-TW`. **Found on the way, and decided (D5-12 clarified):** PDFium's attachment API reads an embedded file's PARAMS dictionary and never the file specification's `/Desc`, so a description is read **first-party** by `pdf/ops/EmbeddedFileReader` off the walk `open()` already runs -- the second read in the product that does not go through PDFium, after `TransitionReader` -- joined to the engine's list by position and confirmed by name; an encrypted document's descriptions are not read (D2-6). No command, no `Commands.def` row: **`mutation-lint` one command** (D5-1). No action registered or minted. **Measured (guide A-76 … A-79):** the label's nine words under six switch settings each, **54 searches**, each found exactly when its class is included, and the three `notFound` words (`wombat`, the `.txt` attachment's content `hedgehog`, the nested PDF's `pangolin`) found by none; the comment and the widget at the generator's rectangles; A-79 over the real file, **3 part reads in all**, every one on the thread page text was extracted on; A-77 through the composition and the find bar from the foot of the page -- the note and the widget brought on screen, the bookmark's `/Fit` showing the whole page, the attachment hit opening no tab and moving neither scroll nor zoom. `ctest` **1308/1308 on `x64-release` and on `x64-debug`** (+16: `tst_search` 2, `tst_index` 4, the scheduler's engine-task cases 2, `tst_embeddedfiles` 3, `tst_searchclasses` 4, A-77's `ui.composition.` case 1) with the same **four named skips**; `gates` **132/132**; `checks` clean, **`layer-lint` 14 escapes**; `rawpage_ui_tests.exe` whole on both presets, **396 cases, 3 skipped** (seeds 183507696 and 198869612); a clean `x64-debug` build, its log kept, **0 warnings and 0 errors over 306 objects** (T7's 303 plus `EmbeddedFileReader.cpp`, `tst_embeddedfiles.cpp`, `tst_searchclasses.cpp`); `--list-actions` **373 declared, 104 registered, 269 not**, unmoved. *(The re-ask on an eviction was changed after the first full release run -- a slot it finds complete is now submitted rather than left -- and every figure here is from the runs after it.)* **Fifteen planted controls, every one failed, each for its own reason** (read from `LastTest.log`): the class switch ignored; PDFDocEncoding as Latin-1; `cancelDocument` blind to tasks; every part read again after each search; an attachment written to the temporary directory; a unit hit with no rectangle; the task run on the caller's thread; the field dedupe removed; the attached PDF's own attachments read; the bookmark hit not navigating; the attachment hit navigating; descriptions never attached; the re-ask on a dropped piece removed; a selection including units; U+FFFD matchable. **None stayed green on its first plant.** One case was added before the plants, because no case reached it: a slot whose text is dropped while its unit part is held on the engine thread (`unit.a search waiting for a page's units asks again for its text when the text is dropped meanwhile`). **Deviations:** (a) **`searchUnits` takes a part** where the plan wrote `searchUnits(handle)`, and A-79's *"exactly one"* is one read per part -- D5-12 clarified, the guide's result says so; (b) **`pdf/ops/EmbeddedFileReader` is a file the plan did not list** -- PDFium cannot read `/Desc`; (c) **`RenderScheduler` gains a third kind of job** the plan did not name, the means of (a); (d) the find bar has **no switches of its own for the four classes** -- they are Preferences' Search page, which it follows as it follows its three toggles; (e) A-76's corpus half is **`tests/render/tst_searchclasses.cpp`**, not `tst_search`, which has no engine. Guide A-76 … A-79 ✅. `Architecture-FileMap.md` (two rows added, both ✅ -- census **184 ✅, 24 🟡, 110 ○, 1 ⤵ of 319** -- ten rows updated, flow G), the plan (D5-12 clarified, §4), the guide, `README.md` and `CLAUDE.md` updated. |
| **T9** Advanced search | ✅ 2026-10-01 — **a folder searched with the UI thread opening nothing; D5-13 clarified; the bench's folder column NOT inside §3.1's budget** (see below) | **The open in pieces** (D5-13 as amended at T3): `IDocumentBackend` gains `beginOpen`/`continueOpen`/`abandonOpen` and `OpenProgress`, and `DocumentBackendImpl::open()` IS that open run in one call -- one open, so every case that opens a real document covers the pieces. `PdfiumDocument::Opening` stages the engine's half (the load with its password loop, the form environment, the count, **the page walk stopping once 1 ms has passed**, the readers, the first-party repair reading with no engine) and `openBytes` runs it to the end; `DocumentBackendImpl` reads the file and assembles (identity, first-party walk, permissions, the handle) with no engine. **`RenderScheduler`**: an engine task may be of NO document (an open has no handle yet), and an **exclusive** task never runs beside a job of its document -- how a folder search closes a file without a drain. **`core/text/FolderSearch`** (a file the plan did not list): the folder listed on a helper thread of its own, then one file at a time -- each ENGINE piece a task at `Background`, every other piece on the helper -- attached to `DocumentIndex` under its identity and searched by `SearchEngine` (all five classes, the flags, what is left of `search/maxResults`), then detached and closed by an exclusive task; never a tab, never a password (an encrypted file is `PermissionDenied` and listed as skipped); `cancel()` stops at once and the piece in flight cleans up after itself. **`core/text/SearchResults`**: relevance DEFINED as the file's hit count, ties by path; the page and file sorts; the filter by author, subject and an inclusive date (`/ModDate` else `/CreationDate`); refine (`hitPages`, `keepOnly`); snippets five whole words either side, never an unmapped glyph; the CSV -- UTF-8 with a byte-order mark, RFC 4180, CRLF. **`ui/panes/SearchPane`**: a dock along the right, `Ctrl+Shift+F` (`find.advancedSearch`, §8's Global row since D5-15) showing it with the focus in its query; `KeyContext::FindSearch`, and `MainWindow` routes `Enter`, `F3`, `Shift+F3` and `Esc` to the pane when the focus is in it; results streamed per file, sorted, filtered, refined, the files not searched listed with their reason, the match drawn bold; **the export through the stage's one planned waiver**, `layer-lint` **14 → 15**. A result opened goes through `MainWindow::openSearchResult` → `openFiles` (not twice) → the tab's `FindReplaceBar::presentHits`. **`find.advancedSearch` registered -- 105 registered, 268 unregistered of 373**, no id minted. 33 catalog keys in `en` and `zh-TW` (30 `search.pane.*`, three `error.search.*`). **Measured:** A-81 *"33 files, 24 searched, 4 skipped as encrypted, 5 broken"*; A-88 225 pieces over 33 files, every engine piece on the engine thread and none on the calling thread; **the 900-page open's longest engine piece 2.76-2.92 ms against the longest page extraction 3.31-3.59 ms** (three runs). **`bench_pipeline --stage5`, the T8 commit's bench against T9's, the same hour (viewport p95, 256 MiB): viewer 15.09 / 15.53, index build 18.73-18.89 / 19.31-19.90, folder search 21.05 / 19.41 ms (24 MiB 22.26 / 19.24), its worst tile 32.7-33.5 / 24.4-25.5 ms, the longest engine hold by an open 884-949 / 8.9-9.9 ms, inversions 0** -- the folder search no longer the outlier (+3.9 over the viewer, where T8 gave +6.0), **but not inside 16.667 ms, and no column with work behind it is**: the viewer alone read 15.1-15.9 ms that day on both binaries where T3 measured 13.02-13.07, which this task did not explain -- recorded in `Tech-Stack-Plan.md` §3.3 and **left to the user with T14**. The longest engine pieces are single page LOADS that resolve a non-embedded font (8-15 ms on `cjk-readable.pdf`, `font-not-embedded.pdf`), which PDFium cannot split. `ctest` **1328/1328 on `x64-release`** (+20: `tst_searchresults` 6, the scheduler's exclusive case 1, `tst_foldersearch` 7, `tst_searchpane` 5, A-80's `ui.composition.` case 1) **and on `x64-debug`**, the same four named skips; `gates` **132/132**; `checks` clean, **`layer-lint` 15 escapes**, `mutation-lint` one command; `rawpage_ui_tests.exe` whole on both presets, **401 cases, 3 skipped** (seeds 1077021396 and 2759673207); a clean `x64-debug` build, its log kept, **0 warnings and 0 errors over 312 objects** (T8's 306 plus the six new translation units). **Planted controls, fourteen, every one failed for its own reason** (read from each run's log): always recursive; a password callback that cancels; `cancel()` doing nothing (re-planted: a first form, which only skipped the generation, would have stayed green behind `running_`); the bound ignored; a snippet cut by a character count; relevance reversed; a file with no author passing an author filter; refine keeping everything; no byte-order mark; engine pieces on the helper; the hits not handed to the opened tab; the pane never seen as focused; the exclusive rule off; and A-09's waiver marker removed. **The 900-page bound failed as first built** (eight pages a piece). **Found by the tests, not planted:** the A-80 composition case crashed on a TEST bug (`Product::settle` refreshing a view the case never set). **Deviations:** (a) the folder search's non-engine pieces run on its own helper thread, so *"every open is on the engine thread"* (A-88) is every ENGINE piece of one; (b) the page walk is bounded by time, not a count; (c) `core/text/FolderSearch` is a file the plan did not list; (d) the export is a `QFile`, not a `QSaveFile` -- `#include <QSaveFile>` is itself a match of the write rule; (e) the bench's folder column is not inside the budget (above). Guide A-80 … A-89 and A-09 ✅. `Architecture-FileMap.md` (two rows added, `SearchPane` ○ → 🟡, census **186 ✅, 25 🟡, 109 ○, 1 ⤵ of 321**, eight rows updated, flow G), `Tech-Stack-Plan.md` §3.3, the plan (D5-13 clarified, §4), the guide, `README.md` and `CLAUDE.md` updated. |
| **T10** Snapshot | ✅ 2026-10-01 — **the snapshot equals a fresh render of its region, 0 pixels differing; D5-11 clarified and its A3 arithmetic corrected** | **`ui/viewer/tools/SnapshotTool`** (D5-11): the marquee's band and click threshold, `S` from §8's Viewer table, a sticky tool like the other four (`ToolKind::Snapshot`, `kToolKindCount` 5); on release it hands the band to the new **`ToolHost::takeSnapshot`**, so the tool names no scheduler. `SnapshotTool::resolve` is the one reading of the new **`pageDisplay/snapshotDpi`** (`SettingsKeys.def`, 0 … 600, default 0): 0 the view's device scale, 72 to 600 dpi, 1 to 71 taken as 72, and **the 50-megapixel bound** reducing to the largest scale that fits and saying from what. **`PageView::snapshotRegionFor`** takes the page the band overlaps most, clipped to it, at that scale, with the request from **`TilePresenter::requestAt`**; a **second `LoupeRaster`** asks for it at `Visible` with **`CachePolicy::Bypass`** (`LoupeRaster` gains the policy and a `failed()` signal); the arrival is composited onto white and put on Qt's clipboard as an image; `snapshotCopied` / `snapshotFailed` go through `DocumentWindow` to the status bar. `tool.snapshot` registered -- **106 registered, 267 unregistered of 373** (`--list-actions`), **no id minted**. Four catalog keys in `en` and `zh-TW`: `shell.status.snapshotCopied`, `…Reduced`, `…Failed` and `prefs.setting.pageDisplay.snapshotDpi` (Page Display's thirteenth row). **Measured:** against a fresh render of the page this suite asks for itself, under `golden/manifest.json`'s thresholds: **0 of 48,000 pixels** as displayed; under the inversion, grid, guides, `Ctrl+A` and the find bar's highlights the VIEW differs in 47,992 of 48,000 and the snapshot in **0**; a 90° view **0 of 435,675**; at 300 dpi **0 of 303,541**, where the as-displayed snapshot enlarged differs in 92,684; a whole A3 page at 600 dpi **5916 × 8366 = 49,493,256 pixels**, reduced and said. `ctest` **1341/1341 on `x64-release` and on `x64-debug`** (+13: `tst_snapshot` 9, `tst_snapshotink` 3 under `ui.composition.`, `tst_stage5settings`' snapshotDpi row 1), the same **four named skips**; `gates` **132/132**; `checks` clean, `mutation-lint` one command, `layer-lint` **15** escapes unchanged; `rawpage_ui_tests.exe` whole on both presets, **410 cases, 3 skipped**; a clean `x64-debug` build, its log kept, **0 `warning:` over 315 objects**. **Planted controls, ten, each fired and restored:** the snapshot inverted with the view, the setting ignored, the bound removed, the floor removed, the cache used, `Esc` taking the snapshot, the tool's click threshold removed, the view's sliver rule removed, the view's rotation dropped, `tool.snapshot` unregistered -- **the tool's threshold STAYED GREEN on its first plant** (the view's sliver rule refuses the same click), and a case driving the tool alone over a counting host was added, which the plant then failed. | **T11** Reflow view | ✅ 2026-10-02 — **`columns-two.pdf` reflowed is the generator's 16 blocks in its reading order; D5-16 clarified** | **`core/text/ReflowText`** (a file the plan did not name, D5-16 clarified (a)): `reflowParagraphs(PageReading)` -- one paragraph per block in the analyzer's order with its page and block, lines joined, a table block a line at a time, runs by size, weight and italic, D5-5's hyphen rule and D5-2's unmapped rule as the copy reads them -- and `blockAtTop`, the first block not wholly above a page view's top edge. **`ui/viewer/ReflowView`**: a read-only `QTextBrowser` built with `QTextCursor`, each paragraph's (page, block) on its text block; a **window of pages** round the place, grown by six as the reader scrolls to an edge and by itself while less than a screen follows the place (capped at 48), read from `DocumentIndex` at `Visible` and rebuilt on a 30 ms coalescing timer with the place kept across rebuilds, resizes and zooms; it declares the `Viewer` context and claims the reading keys through `ShortcutOverride`. **`DocumentWindow::setReflowing`** hides the page surface for it and back -- entering at the block at the active pane's top edge, leaving with the reflow's top block at that edge through the new **`PageView::putPageHeightAtTop`** -- and a find or bookmark hit leaves reflow first, the focus kept unless the reflow had it. `MainWindow`: **`view.reflow`** registered (§8's Viewer `Ctrl+4`, checked while it shows) -- **107 registered, 266 unregistered of 373**, no id minted -- and the zoom chords ask which surface shows: `Ctrl+=`/`Ctrl+-` the text size, Actual Size and the fits its natural size. Two plants were mis-written and failed to compile on their first run; corrected, both fired. Guide **A-92, A-93, A-94 ✅**; **M-09 walkable and not walked.** *(as of that task; every Stage 5 manual case passed by 2026-10-04, `Stage5-TextSearch-Testing-Guide.md` §6)* **Deviations:** **(a) `core/text/ReflowText` is a new core file** -- the paragraph builder is pure and is tested headless -- with a FileMap row added beside `ui/viewer/ReflowView`'s (census **188 ✅, 25 🟡, 109 ○, 1 ⤵ of 323**). **(b) the ink half is `tests/composition/tst_reflowink.cpp`**, for T5's reason. **(c) D5-16 clarified** (`Stage5-TextSearch-Plan.md` §3): a window of pages with an automatic-growth cap, the place at the page view's exact top edge, the reading keys the reflow's, the fits and Actual Size the text's natural size, and the find bar's current hit counting as an activation. **(d)** `tst_viewercommands`' Viewer census 31/8 → **32/7**; `tst_shell`'s and `tst_documenttabs`' counts 106 → 107; `tst_documentchrome`'s example of an unregistered command moved from `view.reflow` to `nav.nextComment`. | **T12** `DocumentComparer`, measured | ✅ 2026-10-02 — **every labelled change of `corpus/compare/` found with its kind, and at text only nothing else; I-07 raised and closed (S2); D5-17 clarified** | **`core/text/DocumentComparer`** (D5-17), pure `core/`: each side a list of `ComparePage` -- a `PageReading`, the crop box, the rotation, the label. **Pages aligned first** -- body hashes diffed for anchors, the leftovers between anchors paired in order by the Dice similarity of their body words, a leftover anywhere paired with one anywhere else as moved, the rest of a gap paired by position -- then **each pair's words diffed** by Myers in linear space (diff-match-patch's `bisect`) under the cap (`replaceShare` of the words, `maxEdits`), hunks split by region, a deleted run equal to an inserted one of at least `minMoveTokens` words a MOVE, tried at each slide of a pure run; at *text and layout* shifted words, with `ignoreWhitespace` off a measured change of spaces; at *appearance* 72 dpi rasters from a `RasterProvider` compared outside every reported rectangle and every ignored word, clustered. **Objectives.** Text becomes first-class: extract it, select it, copy it, find it, search across
files, and diff two documents.

**Components & responsibilities.**
- `core/text/PageTextModel` — `Result<void> hydrate(ITextExtractor&, PageIndex)`. *(As built, T2: the
  model is FILLED by `ITextExtractor::extractPage(handle, page, model&)` on the engine thread's text job,
  and has no `hydrate` of its own.)*
- `core/text/LayoutAnalyzer` — `Blocks analyze(const PageTextModel&, const TagTree*) const`. *(As built,
  T4: `static PageLayout analyze(const PageTextModel&, const TagTree*)` — lines, blocks, reading order,
  tables and columns — with the tree null for every Stage 5 caller, D5-6.)*
- `core/text/TextNormalizer` *(T4, D5-5 — not in this list as first written)*: the one normalization
  pipeline, with an offset map back to the model's characters.
- `core/text/TextSelection` — `Range hitTest(QPointF) const`, `Range expandTo(Granularity) const`,
  `QString asPlainText() const`, `QString asFormatted() const`, `Table asTable() const`. *(As built, T5:
  hit-testing is `PageReading::caretAt(PdfPoint)` in page space and expansion `unitAt(page, point,
  Granularity)`; ONE `copy(const DocumentText&)` returns the plain text, the HTML and — for a column —
  the table together, because one `Ctrl+C` writes every flavour, D5-9.)*
- `core/text/SearchEngine` — `Hits find(const Document&, const SearchQuery&)`,
  `Hits findAcross(const QStringList& paths, const SearchQuery&)`. *(As built, T6: a pure `compile()` and
  `findOnPage()`, and an asynchronous `start(entry, query, handlers)` over one `DocumentIndex` entry,
  matched on a pool of its own and delivered on the pumping thread -- D5-3's "never the UI thread or the
  engine thread"; `findAcross` is T9's folder search.)*
- `core/text/SearchQuery.h`, `core/text/DocumentIndex`. *(As built, T6: the index is keyed by `DocumentId`
  and shared by every window over a file, holds each page's model, reading and normalized forms, and is
  where `SelectionOverlay` reads its pages from too -- D5-4.)*
- `core/text/DocumentComparer` — `DiffSet compare(const Document&, const Document&, CompareOptions)`.
  *(As built, T12: `ComparisonResult compare(const CompareDocument&, const CompareDocument&,
  const CompareOptions&, const RasterProvider&, cancel, progress)` over each side's `PageReading`s -- the
  engine thread's text, not a `Document` -- with the 72 dpi rasters asked for on the comparer's own thread,
  and `ComparisonRun` running it there and delivering on the pumping thread, D5-17.)*
- `pdf/render/PdfiumTextExtractor`. *(`pdf/cos/ContentStreamParser` was listed here and **moved to
  Stage 10** on 2026-09-29 — `Stage5-TextSearch-Plan.md` D5-19: extraction is PDFium's, so its only
  Stage 5 caller would have been a test; `RedactionOps` is its first product caller.)*
- `ui/viewer/SelectionOverlay`, `ui/viewer/ComparisonView`, `ui/panes/SearchPane`,
  `ui/panes/DifferencesPane`, `ui/write/FindReplaceBar` (find half only),
  `ui/viewer/tools/SnapshotTool`, `ui/dialogs/CompareDialog`.
- *(`platform/win/WinRichClipboard` was listed here. **Not built at Stage 5** — `Stage5-TextSearch-Plan.md`
  D5-10, the user's answer to Q3 on 2026-09-29: Qt already publishes text, HTML and bitmaps, and the vector
  snapshot is `DW-2` in `Deferred-Work.md`. The file is **Stage 12's**, whose entry lists it for the paste path.)*

**Key bindings handled** — the whole [Find / Search](./Features.md#find--search) table
(`KeyContext::Search`): `Ctrl+F`, `F3`/`Shift+F3`, `Ctrl+G`/`Ctrl+Shift+G`, `Enter`,
`↑`/`↓`, `Alt+R`, `Alt+W`, `Alt+C`, `Esc` — and [Global](./Features.md#global-active-in-every-context)
`Ctrl+Shift+F`, **moved there from the Find / Search table on 2026-09-29** so Advanced Search opens
from the page (`Stage5-TextSearch-Plan.md` D5-15).
[Viewer](./Features.md#viewer-review-mode): `Alt`+drag (column selection, added 2026-09-29, D5-8),
`Ctrl+Alt+C` (compare), `Ctrl+]`/`Ctrl+[` (next/previous difference), and — **moved here from Stage 4
on 2026-09-18** (`Stage4-ViewingSurface-Plan.md` D4-15) — **`Ctrl+4`** (Reflow view, which needs this
stage's `LayoutAnalyzer`) and **`S`** (Snapshot, whose `SnapshotTool` this stage's component list already named — and which
puts a bitmap on the clipboard through Qt, at `pageDisplay/snapshotDpi`, D5-10 and D5-11).
[Common](./Features.md#common-all-contexts-unless-a-context-above-overrides-the-chord): `Ctrl+C`,
`Ctrl+A` in the Viewer context.

**Data / settings keys.** `search/wholeWords`, `search/caseSensitive`, `search/ignoreDiacritics`,
`search/useRegex`, `search/includeComments`, `search/includeBookmarks`, `search/includeFields`,
`search/includeAttachments`, `search/maxResults`, `search/indexCacheMB`,
`documents/compareSensitivity`, `documents/compareIgnoreHeaderFooter`,
`documents/compareIgnoreWhitespace`, and **`documents/compareIgnorePageNumbers`** — added 2026-09-29
(`Stage5-TextSearch-Plan.md` D5-20), because `Features.md` §1.5 names page numbers apart from
headers and footers and a renumbered document changes one without the other; and
**`pageDisplay/snapshotDpi`** — added 2026-09-29 with the answer to Q3 (D5-11): the snapshot's resolution,
0 meaning as displayed.

**Cross-cutting additions.** No document mutation. i18n: `search.*`, `compare.*`.

**Algorithms & notes.**
- `LayoutAnalyzer` uses the tag tree where present and falls back to geometry *(at Stage 5, geometry
  only: `core/doc/TagTree` is Stage 7's, D5-6)*: baseline clustering
  into lines, gap analysis into blocks, whitespace-column detection, and ruling-line-plus-alignment
  detection for tables. **Its accuracy is measured, not asserted** — a labelled corpus subset with a
  precision/recall target, so Stage 13 inherits a known quantity rather than a hope.
- The document index is built lazily on first search and reused, so a second search of a 900-page
  file is instant.
- Comparison aligns text with a Myers diff over normalized run tokens, then confirms
  page-appearance differences with a raster compare — text-only diffing misses moved images, and
  raster-only diffing reports every reflowed line as a change.

**Testing.**
*Automated:* `tst_selection` (hit-test, word/line/block expansion, column selection, RTL, vertical
text), `tst_layout` (precision/recall against the labelled subset), `tst_search` (all flags, regex,
each content class, CJK, combining marks), `tst_index`, `tst_compare` (known before/after pairs).
*Manual:* → queued **`Stage5-TextSearch-Testing-Guide.md`** (see §5), whose manual section is brief:
selection across columns and page boundaries; copy into Word and confirm formatting;
snapshot into an image editor; search a CJK document; compare two revisions — **on GENERATED
documents, by the user's decision of 2026-09-29** (`Stage5-TextSearch-Plan.md` D5-23): no Stage 5 case
meets a real document.

**Exit criteria.** Selection is accurate on multi-column, RTL, and CJK pages; every §1.8 search
toggle works and is honoured across all five content classes; cross-file search returns correct
snippets; comparison finds text, appearance, and page-level changes on real revision pairs — **met on
GENERATED revision pairs, by the user's decision of 2026-09-29** (`Stage5-TextSearch-Plan.md` D5-23),
so the claim is bounded to the generated corpus and a real pair first meets the comparer after Stage 5.

**Deferrals.** Replace (needs Write mode, Stage 13). Catalog index (Stage 16). The comparison's **summary
page** and **annotated PDF report** (Stage 8, taken 2026-09-29 — D5-17). A **vector** snapshot (`DW-2`,
unassigned — D5-10). OCR'd-layer search
works automatically once Stage 16 adds OCR — no change needed here.

| Task | State | What landed, what was measured, and what deviated |
|---|---|---|
| **T1** The corpus this stage can be judged on | ✅ 2026-10-05 — **two S3 findings, I-01 and I-02, both closed** | **One generator, one model, four outputs.** `corpus/make-annots.py` writes each annotation from a single Python spec into its PDF dictionary, its `/AP`, its label entry and its FDF/XFDF form — so a label cannot describe an annotation the file lacks, nor an exchange file drift from the PDF it targets (plan §1: *the oracle for content is the generator*). **Eleven PDFs** in `ordinary/` — `annots-every-type` (every subtype Stage 6 writes, each with our `/AP`), `annots-no-ap` (nine types without `/AP`, and the same with), `annots-threads` (three authors, replies to replies, a `/RT /Group` replace, Review and Marked history across four time zones; an `/IRT` cycle, a missing parent and a cross-page reply as controls), `annots-flags`, `annots-rotated`, `annots-direct`, `annots-unknown-keys`, `annots-900` (**9,000** annotations, shared appearances, object streams, 0.7 MB), `attachments`, `measure-scale` and `annots-report-v1` (Stage 5's `report-v1` pages byte-identical in content, annotated) — **twelve labels** in `labels/` (`annots-migration.json` says where each of `report-v1`'s ten annotations must land in `report-v2`, or that it must be reported), **six exchange files** (`every-type` and `threads` as FDF and XFDF in Acrobat's shapes, a truncated XFDF, an XFDF declaring an external entity) and **two stamp images** (an RGBA PNG, a baseline JPEG). `make-corpus.py` verifies the new kinds as data and rows them in `PROVENANCE.md`; `corpus/fixturecheck.py`'s `check_annots` is A-15's byte check, expanding object streams and holding every label entry to the object it names. **Measured:** `--verify` **152 files** byte-identical (121 before, none moved); `--check-provenance` **116 rows** (108 PDFs and 8 data files) and 22 labels resolving; `--self-test` **14 of 14** controls, the four new ones each matched to the file it damages; `--check-faults` clean; `--check-manifest` *"108 corpus files, 103 with baselines and 5 excluded"*; **54 baselines** in one filtered capture, **every page looked at first** in a scratch golden root, no tracked PNG changed; `ctest` **1422/1422 on `x64-release` and on `x64-debug`**, the same four named skips (+0: the corpus is checked by existing gate cases, whose expectations moved — `gates.corpus.self_test` to *14 of 14*, `gates.corpus.faults` to *Stage 5 and Stage 6*), and the corpus-walking cases — round trip, reversibility, extraction, golden — now run over the eleven; `gates` **133/133**; `checks` clean, **`mutation-lint` one command** (D6-1's count on the stage's first day). A throwaway probe confirmed the label-to-object check fires, on a loose object and on one inside `annots-900.pdf`'s object stream. Guide A-13 … A-16 ✅. **Findings:** **I-01 (S3)** — `corpus/deflate.py`'s length limiter could leave an **incomplete** Huffman code, which zlib refuses; `annots-900.pdf`'s 3.3 MB object stream (an 18-deep tree) reached it and the encoder's self-check stopped generation. A first fix moved `image-flate-predictor.pdf`'s bytes, so the original repair is kept whenever its code is complete and JPEG's `Adjust_BITS` is used only when it is not — no earlier file moved. **I-02 (S3)** — the capture of `annots-no-ap.pdf` showed **PDFium 153 generates no appearance for a `Line`**; D6-6, guide §3.3 and A-65 listed it among the types PDFium can generate, and now name eight. **Deviations:** **(a)** the corpus has two new directories, `exchange/` and `stamps/`, and `PROVENANCE.md` rows for `.fdf`, `.xfdf`, `.png` and `.jpg` as well as `.pdf`; **(b)** `make-compare.render_page` gained an optional line recorder, drawing nothing; **(c)** three fixtures were corrected from what their pictures showed — a caret over a letter, a reply icon over the line above, two measurement readouts overprinting; **(d)** no fixture carries CJK typed text: a substituted face is recorded per document and would make a fixture's baseline machine-dependent (Stage 5 T1's finding), and T7 measures the CID path (D6-7); **(e)** the control for `attachments.pdf` is the existing `text-a4.pdf`, not a new file. `PROVENANCE.md`, `golden/manifest.json` and `README.md`, `Architecture-FileMap.md` (one row added, ✅ — census **196 ✅, 25 🟡, 107 ○, 1 ⤵ of 329** — and `deflate.py`'s row), the plan (D6-6), the guide, `README.md` and `CLAUDE.md` updated. |
| **T2** The model and its first-party reading | ✅ 2026-10-05 — **A-17 … A-22 ✅, A-23's Windows half; two S3 issues found in T1's work, I-03 and I-04, both closed** | **The model** (`core/doc/Annotation`): subtype, normalized rectangle, geometry as written, colour, opacity, border, text fields, `PdfDate` (raw and UTC, integer arithmetic, no time-zone database -- convention 24), flags, thread and state fields, and `AnnotationId` (object number; page and position for a direct dictionary; `/NM` when created -- D6-4), with a JSON round trip exact for every subtype. **The store** (`core/doc/AnnotationStore`, read side): each page's `/Annots` LISTING entry by entry beside the annotations once per dictionary, a shared one owned by its `/P`'s page; threads per D6-12, built iteratively, with a missing parent, a cross-page reply and every member of a cycle reported and top-level; Review and Marked histories in UTC order, checkmarks per author; queries by page, author, subtype, text and hidden, sorted by page, date, author or subtype; a store that could not be read says why. **The reader** (`pdf/ops/AnnotationReader`, the third first-party read): off the walk the backend already runs, `/Rect` read exactly as PDFium reads it, tolerant and bounded (200,000 coordinates per annotation), every oddity a reported note, an annotation listed by no page reported and ignored. **D6-3's agreement**: `PdfiumDocument::annotationFacts()` takes each page's entries as the engine reads them during the page walk; `Permissions` gains `Operation::Annotate`, `assessAnnotations()` and `mayAnnotate(page)`, refusing a disagreeing page by `error.edit.annotationsDisagree` (both catalogs) with the page named; `IDocumentBackend::annotations()` hands out the store hydrated at open, and the open logs what the reading met. **`fuzz_annot_pipeline`** (D6-29) and its body; the fuzz count **4 → 5**, two `gates.fuzz_count.*` cases; `ci.yml` and the soak run it. **Measured:** A-17, **151 labelled annotations from 11 files equal to the generator's labels** in every field; A-18, **98 files, 3,014 pages, 9,147 `/Annots` entries, 0 pages disagreeing**, ten awkward entry forms read alike by both readers entry by entry, and a page with a too-long number (which the two DO read differently) opened read-only through the backend; the reading costs **27 ms on `annots-900.pdf`** and 0.24 ms on a 900-page file with no annotation, and the engine's facts nothing measurable (both 900-page opens 102 ms; release, medians of 7); A-22 on release, a 1,000,000-point `/InkList` in **205-230 ms**, a 10,000-deep chain in 26 ms, a 10,000-member cycle in 24-25 ms; the fuzz body replayed over **108 corpus files, 101 walked, 9,146 annotations**. `ctest` **1457/1457 on `x64-release` and on `x64-debug`** (+35: `tst_annotation` 9, `tst_annotstore` 13, `tst_annotreader` 9, `tst_permissions` 2, `gates.fuzz_count.*` 2), the same four named skips; `gates` **135/135**; `checks` clean -- `layer-lint` 519 files, `i18n-lint` 320, **`mutation-lint` one command** (T2 builds no command); `rawpage_ui_tests.exe` whole on `x64-debug`, 440 cases, 3 skipped (seed 2551331321). **Planted controls, ten, all fired** (P1 ownership, P2 cycle, P3 current review, P4 per-entry comparison, P5 author bytes, P6 dropped entry, P7 engine entry, P8 bound, P9 the page refusal, P10 the backend's assessment) -- **two only after a change**: P1 did not fail the corpus case (that fixture's `/P` is its first listing page) and failed the thousand-page case; P3 stayed green until its case checked every history rather than one whose history had a single entry. **One intermittent red**, on `x64-release` under `-j 8` in the first full run: Stage 5's A-88 (`render.a 900-page file's open never holds the engine longer than one page's extraction`), engine piece max 4.850 ms against extraction max 4.584 ms; alone it passed five of five (piece max 2.84-3.16 ms, p95 1.16-1.19 ms), and the next full run passed -- the same shape Stage 5 recorded; the log is kept. **Findings:** **I-03 (S3)** -- T1's fixtures wrote `/StateModel` and `/State` as names where ISO 32000-1 Table 172 makes them text strings; the generator writes strings, the reader accepts both. **I-04 (S3)** -- T1's commit left `gates.text_bytes.tracked_tree` red on every preset: `.fdf` (raw PCM in a stream) and `.jpg` were binary types the check did not know, and T1 ran the gates before `git add`; both extensions added. **A defect of a test, not the product**: one `tst_annotreader` case compared iterators of two temporaries and crashed only when it did not run first -- found running the tag whole in both orders. **Deviations:** (a) `IDocumentBackend::annotations()` arrives at T2, not T3, because the reader's own cases need it; (b) A-18's plant is on `annots-threads.pdf` page 2 -- `annots-mixed.pdf`'s page 2 has no annotation; (c) `annots-direct.pdf` gains an annotation no page lists, reached through a hidden state reply's `/IRT`, which A-19 needs and T1 had not made -- no pixel moved; (d) a too-long number cannot become NaN or infinite through `pdf/cos/` (the lexer clamps), so A-22's non-finite rectangle is held at the model and the reader's branch is defensive; (e) A-22's one-second bound is graded on `x64-release`, debug under a stated five-second ceiling and `portable-asan` under a ten-second one -- added after CI run 37250978598 failed that build's 2571 ms against the second, because `RelWithDebInfo` defines `NDEBUG` (guide **I-05**, S3, closed 2026-10-05); hostile serialized input refused under `AnnotationCommandLimits`. **`Command::permitted(document)`**, a new virtual with a default (`core/cmd/Command.cpp`), is the pre-flight `CommandJournal::run` now asks: an annotation command adds `mayAnnotate(page)` for each page it touches, so D6-3's read-only page is refused BY KEY (`error.edit.annotationsDisagree`). **The sink** (`pdf/ops/MutationSinkImpl`): the desired `/Annots` listing per page, only where it differs from the disk -- references, direct entries as written, created annotations -- so add-then-undo writes nothing; `adopt()` finds each created annotation by `/NM`, so an undo after a save lists the SAME object; a removed direct entry is kept by its open-time name. **`pdf/ops/AnnotationOps`**: the created dictionary (D6-4's keys and every set field, one field table) and `editedDictionary` -- **D6-5**, from the dictionary on disk, overwriting only changed modelled keys, `/RC` rewritten exactly when `/Contents` changes and one exists, `/AP` never. **`pdf/ops/AppearanceStreamBuilder`**: Highlight -- a multiply form over the `/Rect`, corners drawn around their centre. **`pdf/cos/ContentStreamBuilder`**: one method per operator, numbers spelled in one place, and `check()` -- Annex A's operators, balanced `q`/`Q` (28 deep) and `BT`/`ET`, no tolerated token -- which every generated stream passes (D6-6). **`RevisionWriter::verify`** holds every page's listing to the sink's (`MutationSinkImpl::reconcile`, strict). **`fuzz_annot_pipeline`'s body** gained D6-29's APPEARANCE and WRITE invariants (a highlight added, page 1 emptied, composed and verified), with a control that fires. **Measured:** A-24, one highlight on `text-a4.pdf` = **exactly 3 objects** (page, annotation, form); the shipped engine reads every highlight we saved on five corpus files as the first-party reader does. **The model hash's cost, measured and fixed in the task:** `CommandJournal::run` hashes the model before every command, and hashing `annots-900.pdf`'s 9,000 annotations' JSON took **78 ms** -- one highlight through the journal took 80 ms; | **T4** How it reaches the pixels — A2 at Stage 6 | ✅ 2026-10-05 — **A-35, A-37, A-38 ✅ -- A-38 a VERDICT, *A2 at Stage 6* `go, with named custom work` (the user's choice), its named work built in the task and the figures still OUTSIDE the guide's §3.1, recorded; A-34, A-36, A-39 in part; one S3 issue, I-06, closed** | **D6-2's reload** (`pdf/render/PdfiumDocument::show`): an edit's `original ⧺ update` is queued and swapped in by the next engine call for the document, **under the engine lock** -- so between jobs, on the render thread -- read through `FPDF_LoadCustomDocument` across the two buffers, the original SHARED with the backend; a newer `show` coalesces, a load the engine refuses keeps the old document, the page tree and readers are not re-read, and the rasterizer corrects for a reloaded `/Rotate` (an unsaved rotation's delta is relative to the tree). **The show step** (`CommandJournal::setShowHandler`, `IDocumentBackend::showEdits`, `DocumentWindow`): after every run, undo and redo, before anything is recorded, the backend composes the update and VERIFIES it as Save does -- so **an edit Save could not write is refused when it is made**, `error.edit.notShown` (both catalogs), taken back with no trace; companions are shown the leader's edits and a detached one goes back on its file. **D6-10's aspect** (`core/doc/Scope`): an annotation scope drops tiles and keeps the page's text (`DocumentIndex`, told as an eviction). **The verdict and its named work** -- `bench_pipeline --stage6` (D6-24), the scroll's tiles' wait split into queued / in the engine / waiting for the pump: the first measurement read the viewport p95 at **112.5-114.7 ms** and edit-to-pixels p95 at **14.7-15.4 s**, every file outside, with the reload costing p95 1.9-2.0 ms -- the cost was the render thread redrawing the edited page's 40 viewport tiles after each edit (23 of 30 s) and every revision cancelling every queued tile of the document. Asked, **the user chose `go, with named custom work` and the work now**: **an annotation scope carries each changed annotation's padded `/Rect`** (the whole page for a popup, an annotation with one, `NoZoom`, `NoRotate` or a rectangle that is no box) and the cache and the scheduler drop only the tiles it reaches (`core/render/tileTouchedBy`, through the engine's page tree), **and a revision re-keys every queued, running or finished tile it does not reach** (I-06). Three graded runs after it: redrawn tiles **528-586 a run against ~7,400**; viewport p95 **pages-900 49.6 / 96.4 / 105.7 ms, text-900 99.5 / 99.6 / 107.6, objstm-xrefstream 0.05 / 0.56 / 53.4**; edit-to-pixels p95 **47 / 49 / 301, 109 / 154 / 301, 43 / 246 / 243 ms**, max 301-360 -- **still outside**, the remaining causes named in `Tech-Stack-Plan.md` §3.3: a tile of a page carrying hundreds of annotations costs p95 38-50 ms by itself (PDFium draws every annotation of a page in every tile), and compose and verify on the UI thread grow with the unsaved annotations (`journal.run` p95 47-68 ms by 300) -- each created annotation's objects serialized once is named and not built. **Measured besides:** A-34, the live page and the saved file reopened **0 differing bytes** on nine pages at 72 and 144 dpi and turned 90°, a rotation and a highlight in both orders, and every tile of an edited page through the scheduler (35 tiles, 4-8 redrawn) at three view rotations, on turned pages and under a delta; A-35, an undo **0** from the original; A-36, two tiles from the cache, one redrawn, no text job; A-37, every swap on the worker, no engine entry without the lock, the engine reading **the backend's own buffer**, live heap **78 bytes a reload** against 12,645; A-39, a faulted compose refused by key with the page drawn as before. `ctest` **1503/1503 on `x64-release` and 1503/1503 on `x64-debug`** (+21: `tst_annotdisplay` 9 (`render.`), `bench.render.a2_stage6`, `tst_docvocabulary` 2, `tst_journal` 2, `tst_annotationops` 3, `tst_index` 1, `tst_rendercache` 2, `tst_renderscheduler` 1), the same **four named skips**; `gates` **135/135** on the `gates` preset; `checks` clean, **`mutation-lint` three commands**, `layer-lint` **15** escapes; `rawpage_ui_tests.exe` whole on `x64-debug`, **440 cases, 3 skipped (seed 624762495)**; a clean `x64-debug` build, **0 `warning:` and 0 `error:` over 346 C++ objects**; `--list-actions` **374 / 111 / 263**, unmoved (T4 registers no action). **Planted controls, sixteen, all fired** -- the reload skipped; no `/Rotate` correction; the journal keeping a refused edit; `showEdits` skipping verify; an annotation scope as content; the index dropping an annotated page's text; a rectangle placed without rotation; without the crop box; the scheduler re-keying a tile the change reached (twice: the unit case, and the every-tile case -- which **stayed green at first**, because it delivered every tile before the edit and a re-keyed QUEUED tile is drawn after the swap and so correctly; strengthened to land the edit on finished, undelivered tiles, it fired); a running re-keyed tile delivered under its old revision; the original deep-copied; the reload made on the calling thread; a `NoZoom`/`NoRotate`/popup annotation given its rectangle; a merge keeping rectangles on a page whole on one side; and a refused load swapped in anyway (whose first plant did not compile under `-Werror` and was rewritten -- read from the build's exit code). **Deviations:** **(a)** the edit-time check composes the WHOLE file transiently, as Save's verify does; **(c)** the named work -- rectangles in `Scope`, `tileTouchedBy`, the scheduler's re-key, `Stats::rekeyed`, `documentChanged`'s page tree -- is code the plan's T4 did not list, chosen by the user from the measurement;
